  <x-color><param>#b0d6f5</param>"Return region between //: comments in sclang, as string.
If the beginning of line is '<x-color><param>#00bcff</param>//:+</x-color>', then fork the snippet as routine.
If the beginning of line is '<x-color><param>#00bcff</param>//:*</x-color>', then wrap the snippet in loop and fork."</x-color>
  <x-color><param>#ff62d4</param>(</x-color><x-color><param>#b6a0ff</param>save-excursion</x-color>

</x-color>    <x-color><param>#3fdfd0</param>(</x-color><x-color><param>#b6a0ff</param>let</x-color> <x-color><param>#fba849</param>(</x-color>
          <x-color><param>#9f80ff</param>(</x-color>snippet-begin <x-color><param>#4fe42f</param>(</x-color>search-backward-regexp <x-color><param>#79a8ff</param>"^//:"</x-color> nil t<x-color><param>#4fe42f</param>)</x-color><x-color><param>#9f80ff</param>)</x-color>
          snippet-end
          snippet
          snippet-head
          <x-color><param>#9f80ff</param>(</x-color>prefix <x-color><param>#79a8ff</param>""</x-color><x-color><param>#9f80ff</param>)</x-color><x-color><param>#fba849</param>)</x-color>

        <x-color><param>#9f80ff</param>(</x-color><x-color><param>#b6a0ff</param>setq</x-color> snippet-begin <x-color><param>#4fe42f</param>(</x-color>point-min<x-color><param>#4fe42f</param>)</x-color><x-color><param>#9f80ff</param>)</x-color>
        <x-color><param>#9f80ff</param>(</x-color><x-color><param>#b6a0ff</param>setq</x-color> prefix <x-color><param>#79a8ff</param>"//:\n"</x-color><x-color><param>#9f80ff</param>)</x-color><x-color><param>#fba849</param>)</x-color>

<x-color><param>#ffffff</param>(</x-color><x-color><param>#b6a0ff</param>defun</x-color> <x-color><param>#feacd0</param>sclang-eval-current-snippet</x-color> <x-color><param>#ff62d4</param>()</x-color>
  <x-color><param>#b0d6f5</param>"Evaluate the current snippet in sclang.
A snippet is a block of code enclosed between comments
starting at the beginning of line and with a : following immediately after '<x-color><param>#00bcff</param>//</x-color>'.
If the beginning of line is '<x-color><param>#00bcff</param>//:+</x-color>', then fork the snippet as routine.
If the beginning of line is '<x-color><param>#00bcff</param>//:*</x-color>', then wrap the snippet in loop and fork."</x-color>
  <x-color><param>#ff62d4</param>(</x-color><x-color><param>#b6a0ff</param>interactive</x-color><x-color><param>#ff62d4</param>)</x-color>
  <x-color><param>#ff62d4</param>(</x-color><x-color><param>#b6a0ff</param>let*</x-color> <x-color><param>#3fdfd0</param>(</x-color>sclang-snippet-is-routine
         sclang-snippet-is-loop
         <x-color><param>#fba849</param>(</x-color>snippet <x-color><param>#9f80ff</param>(</x-color>sclang-get-current-snippet<x-color><param>#9f80ff</param>)</x-color><x-color><param>#fba849</param>)</x-color><x-color><param>#3fdfd0</param>)</x-color>

    <x-color><param>#3fdfd0</param>(</x-color><x-color><param>#b6a0ff</param>if</x-color> sclang-snippet-is-loop
        <x-color><param>#fba849</param>(</x-color><x-color><param>#b6a0ff</param>setq</x-color> snippet <x-color><param>#9f80ff</param>(</x-color>format <x-color><param>#79a8ff</param>"{\n loop {\n %s \n} \n }.fork"</x-color> snippet<x-color><param>#9f80ff</param>)</x-color><x-color><param>#fba849</param>)</x-color><x-color><param>#3fdfd0</param>)</x-color>

</x-color>   <x-color><param>cyan</param>;; </x-color><x-color><param>gray80</param>This is because sclang blocks must currently be marked as javascript
</x-color>   <x-color><param>cyan</param>;; </x-color><x-color><param>gray80</param>in order to render properly with hugo / pygments for webite creation.
</x-color>   <x-color><param>#3fdfd0</param>(</x-color><x-color><param>#b6a0ff</param>setq</x-color> org-src-lang-modes <x-color><param>#fba849</param>(</x-color>add-to-list 'org-src-lang-modes '<x-color><param>#9f80ff</param>(</x-color><x-color><param>#79a8ff</param>"javascript"</x-color> . sclang<x-color><param>#9f80ff</param>)</x-color><x-color><param>#fba849</param>)</x-color><x-color><param>#3fdfd0</param>)</x-color>

   <x-color><param>cyan</param>;; </x-color><x-color><param>gray80</param>(org-indent-mode) ;; this results in added leading spaces in org-edit-special

   <x-color><param>cyan</param>;; </x-color><x-color><param>gray80</param>turn off prelude mode because its key bindings interfere with table bindings.
</x-color>   <x-color><param>cyan</param>;; </x-color><x-color><param>gray80</param>Instead, the prelude-mode keybindings have been copied to org-mode above,
</x-color>   <x-color><param>cyan</param>;; </x-color><x-color><param>gray80</param>minus the unwanted keybindings for tables.

   <x-color><param>cyan</param>;; </x-color><x-color><param>gray80</param>disable whitespace mode, which was previously disabled by prelude-mode

</x-color><x-color><param>cyan</param>;; </x-color><x-color><param>gray80</param>The only entry point is `<x-color><param>#00bcff</param>describe-unbound-keys</x-color>'; it prompts for the maximum

</x-color><x-color><param>cyan</param>;; </x-color><x-color><param>gray80</param>keys to be worthwhile.  Lisp may call just `<x-color><param>#00bcff</param>unbound-keys</x-color>' to get a list of

</x-color>
<x-color><param>cyan</param>;;; </x-color><x-color><param>gray80</param>Commentary:
</x-color>
<x-color><param>cyan</param>;; </x-color><x-color><param>gray80</param>This provides an edit server to respond to requests from the Chrome
</x-color><x-color><param>cyan</param>;; </x-color><x-color><param>gray80</param>Emacs Chrome plugin. This is my first attempt at doing something
</x-color><x-color><param>cyan</param>;; </x-color><x-color><param>gray80</param>with sockets in Emacs. I based it on the following examples:
</x-color><x-color><param>cyan</param>;;</x-color><x-color><param>gray80</param>
</x-color><x-color><param>cyan</param>;;   </x-color><x-color><param>gray80</param>http://www.emacswiki.org/emacs/EmacsEchoServer
</x-color><x-color><param>cyan</param>;;   </x-color><x-color><param>gray80</param>http://nullprogram.com/blog/2009/05/17/
</x-color><x-color><param>cyan</param>;;</x-color><x-color><param>gray80</param>
</x-color><x-color><param>cyan</param>;; </x-color><x-color><param>gray80</param>To use it ensure the file is in your load-path and add something
</x-color><x-color><param>cyan</param>;; </x-color><x-color><param>gray80</param>like the following examples to your .emacs:
</x-color><x-color><param>cyan</param>;;</x-color><x-color><param>gray80</param>
</x-color><x-color><param>cyan</param>;; </x-color><x-color><param>gray80</param>To open pages for editing in new buffers in your existing Emacs
</x-color><x-color><param>cyan</param>;; </x-color><x-color><param>gray80</param>instance:
</x-color><x-color><param>cyan</param>;;</x-color><x-color><param>gray80</param>
</x-color><x-color><param>cyan</param>;;   </x-color><x-color><param>gray80</param>(when (require 'edit-server nil t)
</x-color><x-color><param>cyan</param>;;     </x-color><x-color><param>gray80</param>(setq edit-server-new-frame nil)
</x-color><x-color><param>cyan</param>;;     </x-color><x-color><param>gray80</param>(edit-server-start))
</x-color><x-color><param>cyan</param>;;</x-color><x-color><param>gray80</param>
</x-color><x-color><param>cyan</param>;; </x-color><x-color><param>gray80</param>To open pages for editing in new frames using a running emacs
</x-color><x-color><param>cyan</param>;; </x-color><x-color><param>gray80</param>started in --daemon mode:
</x-color><x-color><param>cyan</param>;;</x-color><x-color><param>gray80</param>
</x-color><x-color><param>cyan</param>;;   </x-color><x-color><param>gray80</param>(when (and (require 'edit-server nil t) (daemonp))
</x-color><x-color><param>cyan</param>;;     </x-color><x-color><param>gray80</param>(edit-server-start))
</x-color><x-color><param>cyan</param>;;</x-color><x-color><param>gray80</param>
</x-color><x-color><param>cyan</param>;; </x-color><x-color><param>gray80</param>Buffers are edited in `<x-color><param>#00bcff</param>text-mode</x-color>' by default; to use a different
</x-color><x-color><param>cyan</param>;; </x-color><x-color><param>gray80</param>major mode, change `<x-color><param>#00bcff</param>edit-server-default-major-mode</x-color>' or customize
</x-color><x-color><param>cyan</param>;; </x-color><x-color><param>gray80</param>`<x-color><param>#00bcff</param>edit-server-url-major-mode-alist</x-color>' to specify major modes based
</x-color><x-color><param>cyan</param>;; </x-color><x-color><param>gray80</param>on the remote URL:
</x-color><x-color><param>cyan</param>;;</x-color><x-color><param>gray80</param>
</x-color><x-color><param>cyan</param>;;   </x-color><x-color><param>gray80</param>(setq edit-server-url-major-mode-alist
</x-color><x-color><param>cyan</param>;;         </x-color><x-color><param>gray80</param>'(("github\\.com" . markdown-mode)))
</x-color><x-color><param>cyan</param>;;</x-color><x-color><param>gray80</param>
</x-color><x-color><param>cyan</param>;; </x-color><x-color><param>gray80</param>Alternatively, set the mode in `<x-color><param>#00bcff</param>edit-server-start-hook</x-color>'.  For
</x-color><x-color><param>cyan</param>;; </x-color><x-color><param>gray80</param>example:
</x-color><x-color><param>cyan</param>;;</x-color><x-color><param>gray80</param>
</x-color><x-color><param>cyan</param>;; </x-color><x-color><param>gray80</param>(add-hook 'edit-server-start-hook
</x-color><x-color><param>cyan</param>;;           </x-color><x-color><param>gray80</param>(lambda ()
</x-color><x-color><param>cyan</param>;;             </x-color><x-color><param>gray80</param>(when (string-match "github.com" (buffer-name))
</x-color><x-color><param>cyan</param>;;               </x-color><x-color><param>gray80</param>(markdown-mode))))
</x-color>

<x-color><param>cyan</param>;;; </x-color><x-color><param>gray80</param>Code:
</x-color>
<x-color><param>cyan</param>;; </x-color><x-color><param>gray80</param>uncomment to debug
</x-color><x-color><param>cyan</param>;; </x-color><x-color><param>gray80</param>(setq debug-on-error t)
</x-color><x-color><param>cyan</param>;; </x-color><x-color><param>gray80</param>(setq edebug-all-defs t)
</x-color>
<x-color><param>#ffffff</param>(</x-color><x-color><param>#b6a0ff</param>unless</x-color> <x-color><param>#ff62d4</param>(</x-color><x-color><param>#b6a0ff</param>featurep</x-color> '<x-color><param>#00bcff</param>make-network-process</x-color><x-color><param>#ff62d4</param>)</x-color>
  <x-color><param>#ff62d4</param>(</x-color><x-color><param>#dbbe5f</param>error</x-color> <x-color><param>#79a8ff</param>"Incompatible version of [X]Emacs - lacks make-network-process"</x-color><x-color><param>#ff62d4</param>)</x-color><x-color><param>#ffffff</param>)</x-color>

<x-color><param>cyan</param>;;; </x-color><x-color><param>gray80</param>Customization
</x-color>
<x-color><param>#ffffff</param>(</x-color><x-color><param>#b6a0ff</param>defcustom</x-color> <x-color><param>#00d3d0</param>edit-server-port</x-color> 9292
  <x-color><param>#b0d6f5</param>"Local port the edit server listens to."</x-color>
  <x-color><param>#f78fe7</param>:group</x-color> 'edit-server
  <x-color><param>#f78fe7</param>:type</x-color> 'integer<x-color><param>#ffffff</param>)</x-color>

<x-color><param>#ffffff</param>(</x-color><x-color><param>#b6a0ff</param>defcustom</x-color> <x-color><param>#00d3d0</param>edit-server-host</x-color> nil
  <x-color><param>#b0d6f5</param>"If not nil, accept connections from HOST address rather than just
localhost. This may present a security issue."</x-color>
  <x-color><param>#f78fe7</param>:group</x-color> 'edit-server
  <x-color><param>#f78fe7</param>:type</x-color> 'boolean<x-color><param>#ffffff</param>)</x-color>

<x-color><param>#ffffff</param>(</x-color><x-color><param>#b6a0ff</param>defcustom</x-color> <x-color><param>#00d3d0</param>edit-server-verbose</x-color> nil
  <x-color><param>#b0d6f5</param>"If not nil, log connections and progress also to the echo area."</x-color>
  <x-color><param>#f78fe7</param>:group</x-color> 'edit-server
  <x-color><param>#f78fe7</param>:type</x-color> 'boolean<x-color><param>#ffffff</param>)</x-color>

<x-color><param>#ffffff</param>(</x-color><x-color><param>#b6a0ff</param>defcustom</x-color> <x-color><param>#00d3d0</param>edit-server-done-hook</x-color> nil
  <x-color><param>#b0d6f5</param>"Hook run when done editing a buffer for the Emacs HTTP edit-server.
Current buffer holds the text that is about to be sent back to the client."</x-color>
  <x-color><param>#f78fe7</param>:group</x-color> 'edit-server
  <x-color><param>#f78fe7</param>:type</x-color> 'hook<x-color><param>#ffffff</param>)</x-color>

<x-color><param>#ffffff</param>(</x-color><x-color><param>#b6a0ff</param>defcustom</x-color> <x-color><param>#00d3d0</param>edit-server-start-hook</x-color> nil
  <x-color><param>#b0d6f5</param>"Hook run when starting a editing buffer.  Current buffer is
the fully prepared editing buffer.  Use this hook to enable
buffer-specific modes or add key bindings."</x-color>
  <x-color><param>#f78fe7</param>:group</x-color> 'edit-server
  <x-color><param>#f78fe7</param>:type</x-color> 'hook<x-color><param>#ffffff</param>)</x-color>

<x-color><param>#ffffff</param>(</x-color><x-color><param>#b6a0ff</param>defcustom</x-color> <x-color><param>#00d3d0</param>edit-server-edit-mode-hook</x-color> nil
  <x-color><param>#b0d6f5</param>"Hook run when we enter edit-server-edit-mode.  This is the final step of
an edit session and is called when all frames and displays have been
set-up.  You should not set any additional major modes here though as they
may conflict with the edit-server-edit-mode, use the
edit-server-start-hook instead."</x-color>
  <x-color><param>#f78fe7</param>:group</x-color> 'edit-server
  <x-color><param>#f78fe7</param>:type</x-color> 'hook<x-color><param>#ffffff</param>)</x-color>

<x-color><param>cyan</param>;; </x-color><x-color><param>gray80</param>frame options
</x-color>
<x-color><param>#ffffff</param>(</x-color><x-color><param>#b6a0ff</param>defcustom</x-color> <x-color><param>#00d3d0</param>edit-server-new-frame</x-color> t
  <x-color><param>#b0d6f5</param>"If not nil, edit each buffer in a new frame (and raise it)."</x-color>
  <x-color><param>#f78fe7</param>:group</x-color> 'edit-server
  <x-color><param>#f78fe7</param>:type</x-color> 'boolean<x-color><param>#ffffff</param>)</x-color>

<x-color><param>#ffffff</param>(</x-color><x-color><param>#b6a0ff</param>defcustom</x-color> <x-color><param>#00d3d0</param>edit-server-new-frame-alist</x-color>
  '<x-color><param>#ff62d4</param>(</x-color><x-color><param>#3fdfd0</param>(</x-color>name . <x-color><param>#79a8ff</param>"Edit with Emacs FRAME"</x-color><x-color><param>#3fdfd0</param>)</x-color>
    <x-color><param>#3fdfd0</param>(</x-color>width . 80<x-color><param>#3fdfd0</param>)</x-color>
    <x-color><param>#3fdfd0</param>(</x-color>height . 25<x-color><param>#3fdfd0</param>)</x-color>
    <x-color><param>#3fdfd0</param>(</x-color>minibuffer . t<x-color><param>#3fdfd0</param>)</x-color>
    <x-color><param>#3fdfd0</param>(</x-color>menu-bar-lines . t<x-color><param>#3fdfd0</param>)</x-color><x-color><param>#ff62d4</param>)</x-color>
  <x-color><param>#b0d6f5</param>"Frame parameters for new frames.  See `<x-color><param>#00bcff</param>make-frame</x-color>' for examples.
You will want to look at the VALUE of '<x-color><param>#00bcff</param>window-system</x-color>', '<x-color><param>#00bcff</param>display</x-color>' and
'<x-color><param>#00bcff</param>terminal</x-color>' if you want to force any particular display type and
not default to whatever is currently open. If nil, the new
frame will use the existing `<x-color><param>#00bcff</param>default-frame-alist</x-color>' values."</x-color>
  <x-color><param>#f78fe7</param>:group</x-color> 'edit-server
  <x-color><param>#f78fe7</param>:type</x-color> '<x-color><param>#ff62d4</param>(</x-color>repeat <x-color><param>#3fdfd0</param>(</x-color>cons <x-color><param>#f78fe7</param>:format</x-color> <x-color><param>#79a8ff</param>"%v"</x-color>
</x-color><x-bg-color><param>#101424</param><x-color><param>#aa9e9f</param>		</x-color></x-bg-color><x-color><param>#ffffff</param>       <x-color><param>#fba849</param>(</x-color>symbol <x-color><param>#f78fe7</param>:tag</x-color> <x-color><param>#79a8ff</param>"Parameter"</x-color><x-color><param>#fba849</param>)</x-color>
</x-color><x-bg-color><param>#101424</param><x-color><param>#aa9e9f</param>		</x-color></x-bg-color><x-color><param>#ffffff</param>       <x-color><param>#fba849</param>(</x-color>sexp <x-color><param>#f78fe7</param>:tag</x-color> <x-color><param>#79a8ff</param>"Value"</x-color><x-color><param>#fba849</param>)</x-color><x-color><param>#3fdfd0</param>)</x-color><x-color><param>#ff62d4</param>)</x-color><x-color><param>#ffffff</param>)</x-color>

<x-color><param>#ffffff</param>(</x-color><x-color><param>#b6a0ff</param>defcustom</x-color> <x-color><param>#00d3d0</param>edit-server-default-major-mode</x-color>
  'text-mode
  <x-color><param>#b0d6f5</param>"The default major mode to use in editing buffers, if no other
mode is selected by `<x-color><param>#00bcff</param>edit-server-url-major-mode-alist</x-color>'."</x-color>
  <x-color><param>#f78fe7</param>:group</x-color> 'edit-server
  <x-color><param>#f78fe7</param>:type</x-color> 'function<x-color><param>#ffffff</param>)</x-color>

<x-color><param>#ffffff</param>(</x-color><x-color><param>#b6a0ff</param>defcustom</x-color> <x-color><param>#00d3d0</param>edit-server-url-major-mode-alist</x-color>
  nil
  <x-color><param>#b0d6f5</param>"A-list of patterns and corresponding functions; when the first
pattern is encountered which matches `<x-color><param>#00bcff</param>edit-server-url</x-color>', the
corresponding function will be called in order to set the desired
major mode. If no pattern matches,
`<x-color><param>#00bcff</param>edit-server-default-major-mode</x-color>' will be used."</x-color>
  <x-color><param>#f78fe7</param>:group</x-color> 'edit-server
  <x-color><param>#f78fe7</param>:type</x-color> '<x-color><param>#ff62d4</param>(</x-color>alist <x-color><param>#f78fe7</param>:key-type</x-color> <x-color><param>#3fdfd0</param>(</x-color>string <x-color><param>#f78fe7</param>:tag</x-color> <x-color><param>#79a8ff</param>"Regexp"</x-color><x-color><param>#3fdfd0</param>)</x-color>
</x-color><x-bg-color><param>#101424</param><x-color><param>#aa9e9f</param>		</x-color></x-bg-color><x-color><param>#f78fe7</param><x-color><param>#ffffff</param>:value-type</x-color></x-color><x-color><param>#ffffff</param> <x-color><param>#3fdfd0</param>(</x-color><x-color><param>#b6a0ff</param>function</x-color> <x-color><param>#f78fe7</param>:tag</x-color> <x-color><param>#79a8ff</param>"Major mode function"</x-color><x-color><param>#3fdfd0</param>)</x-color><x-color><param>#ff62d4</param>)</x-color><x-color><param>#ffffff</param>)</x-color>

<x-color><param>#ffffff</param>(</x-color><x-color><param>#b6a0ff</param>defcustom</x-color> <x-color><param>#00d3d0</param>edit-server-new-frame-mode-line</x-color> t
  <x-color><param>#b0d6f5</param>"Show the emacs frame's mode-line if set to t; hide if nil."</x-color>
  <x-color><param>#f78fe7</param>:group</x-color> 'edit-server
  <x-color><param>#f78fe7</param>:type</x-color> 'boolean<x-color><param>#ffffff</param>)</x-color>

<x-color><param>cyan</param>;;; </x-color><x-color><param>gray80</param>Variables
</x-color>
<x-color><param>#ffffff</param>(</x-color><x-color><param>#b6a0ff</param>defconst</x-color> <x-color><param>#00d3d0</param>edit-server-process-buffer-name</x-color> <x-color><param>#79a8ff</param>" *edit-server*"</x-color>
  <x-color><param>#b0d6f5</param>"Template name of the edit-server process buffers."</x-color><x-color><param>#ffffff</param>)</x-color>

<x-color><param>#ffffff</param>(</x-color><x-color><param>#b6a0ff</param>defconst</x-color> <x-color><param>#00d3d0</param>edit-server-log-buffer-name</x-color> <x-color><param>#79a8ff</param>"*edit-server-log*"</x-color>
  <x-color><param>#b0d6f5</param>"Name of the edit-server process buffers."</x-color><x-color><param>#ffffff</param>)</x-color>

<x-color><param>#ffffff</param>(</x-color><x-color><param>#b6a0ff</param>defconst</x-color> <x-color><param>#00d3d0</param>edit-server-edit-buffer-name</x-color> <x-color><param>#79a8ff</param>"TEXTAREA"</x-color>
  <x-color><param>#b0d6f5</param>"Template name of the edit-server text editing buffers."</x-color><x-color><param>#ffffff</param>)</x-color>

<x-color><param>#ffffff</param>(</x-color><x-color><param>#b6a0ff</param>defvar</x-color> <x-color><param>#00d3d0</param>edit-server-clients</x-color> <x-color><param>#ff62d4</param>()</x-color>
  <x-color><param>#b0d6f5</param>"List of all client processes associated with the server process."</x-color><x-color><param>#ffffff</param>)</x-color>

<x-color><param>cyan</param>;; </x-color><x-color><param>gray80</param>Buffer local variables
</x-color><x-color><param>cyan</param>;;</x-color><x-color><param>gray80</param>
</x-color><x-color><param>cyan</param>;; </x-color><x-color><param>gray80</param>These are all required to associate the edit buffer with the
</x-color><x-color><param>cyan</param>;; </x-color><x-color><param>gray80</param>correct connection to the client and allow for the buffer to be sent
</x-color><x-color><param>cyan</param>;; </x-color><x-color><param>gray80</param>back when ready. They are `permanent-local` to avoid being reset if
</x-color><x-color><param>cyan</param>;; </x-color><x-color><param>gray80</param>the buffer changes major modes.
</x-color>
<x-color><param>#ffffff</param>(</x-color><x-color><param>#b6a0ff</param>defvar</x-color> <x-color><param>#00d3d0</param>edit-server-proc</x-color> nil
  <x-color><param>#b0d6f5</param>"Network process associated with the current edit."</x-color><x-color><param>#ffffff</param>)</x-color>
<x-color><param>#ffffff</param>(</x-color>make-variable-buffer-local 'edit-server-proc<x-color><param>#ffffff</param>)</x-color>
<x-color><param>#ffffff</param>(</x-color>put 'edit-server-proc 'permanent-local t<x-color><param>#ffffff</param>)</x-color>

<x-color><param>#ffffff</param>(</x-color><x-color><param>#b6a0ff</param>defvar</x-color> <x-color><param>#00d3d0</param>edit-server-frame</x-color> nil
  <x-color><param>#b0d6f5</param>"The frame created for a new edit-server process."</x-color><x-color><param>#ffffff</param>)</x-color>
<x-color><param>#ffffff</param>(</x-color>make-variable-buffer-local 'edit-server-frame<x-color><param>#ffffff</param>)</x-color>
<x-color><param>#ffffff</param>(</x-color>put 'edit-server-frame 'permanent-local t<x-color><param>#ffffff</param>)</x-color>

<x-color><param>#ffffff</param>(</x-color><x-color><param>#b6a0ff</param>defvar</x-color> <x-color><param>#00d3d0</param>edit-server-phase</x-color> nil
  <x-color><param>#b0d6f5</param>"Symbol indicating the state of the HTTP request parsing."</x-color><x-color><param>#ffffff</param>)</x-color>
<x-color><param>#ffffff</param>(</x-color>make-variable-buffer-local 'edit-server-phase<x-color><param>#ffffff</param>)</x-color>
<x-color><param>#ffffff</param>(</x-color>put 'edit-server-phase 'permanent-local t<x-color><param>#ffffff</param>)</x-color>

<x-color><param>#ffffff</param>(</x-color><x-color><param>#b6a0ff</param>defvar</x-color> <x-color><param>#00d3d0</param>edit-server-received</x-color> nil
  <x-color><param>#b0d6f5</param>"Number of bytes received so far in the client buffer.
Depending on the character encoding, may be different from the buffer length."</x-color><x-color><param>#ffffff</param>)</x-color>
<x-color><param>#ffffff</param>(</x-color>make-variable-buffer-local 'edit-server-received<x-color><param>#ffffff</param>)</x-color>
<x-color><param>#ffffff</param>(</x-color>put 'edit-server-received 'permanent-local t<x-color><param>#ffffff</param>)</x-color>

<x-color><param>#ffffff</param>(</x-color><x-color><param>#b6a0ff</param>defvar</x-color> <x-color><param>#00d3d0</param>edit-server-request</x-color> nil
  <x-color><param>#b0d6f5</param>"The HTTP request (GET, HEAD, POST) received."</x-color><x-color><param>#ffffff</param>)</x-color>
<x-color><param>#ffffff</param>(</x-color>make-variable-buffer-local 'edit-server-request<x-color><param>#ffffff</param>)</x-color>

<x-color><param>cyan</param>;;; </x-color><x-color><param>gray80</param>Mode magic
</x-color><x-color><param>cyan</param>;;</x-color><x-color><param>gray80</param>
</x-color><x-color><param>cyan</param>;; </x-color><x-color><param>gray80</param>We want to re-map some of the keys to trigger edit-server-done
</x-color><x-color><param>cyan</param>;; </x-color><x-color><param>gray80</param>instead of the usual emacs like behaviour. However using
</x-color><x-color><param>cyan</param>;; </x-color><x-color><param>gray80</param>local-set-key will affect all buffers of the same mode, hence we
</x-color><x-color><param>cyan</param>;; </x-color><x-color><param>gray80</param>define a special (derived) mode for handling editing of text areas.
</x-color>

<x-color><param>#ffffff</param>(</x-color><x-color><param>#b6a0ff</param>defvar</x-color> <x-color><param>#00d3d0</param>edit-server-edit-mode-map</x-color>
  <x-color><param>#ff62d4</param>(</x-color><x-color><param>#b6a0ff</param>let</x-color> <x-color><param>#3fdfd0</param>(</x-color><x-color><param>#fba849</param>(</x-color>map <x-color><param>#9f80ff</param>(</x-color>make-sparse-keymap<x-color><param>#9f80ff</param>)</x-color><x-color><param>#fba849</param>)</x-color><x-color><param>#3fdfd0</param>)</x-color>
    <x-color><param>#3fdfd0</param>(</x-color>define-key map <x-color><param>#fba849</param>(</x-color>kbd <x-color><param>#79a8ff</param>"C-x C-s"</x-color><x-color><param>#fba849</param>)</x-color> 'edit-server-save<x-color><param>#3fdfd0</param>)</x-color>
    <x-color><param>#3fdfd0</param>(</x-color>define-key map <x-color><param>#fba849</param>(</x-color>kbd <x-color><param>#79a8ff</param>"C-x #"</x-color><x-color><param>#fba849</param>)</x-color>   'edit-server-done<x-color><param>#3fdfd0</param>)</x-color>
    <x-color><param>#3fdfd0</param>(</x-color>define-key map <x-color><param>#fba849</param>(</x-color>kbd <x-color><param>#79a8ff</param>"C-c C-c"</x-color><x-color><param>#fba849</param>)</x-color> 'edit-server-done<x-color><param>#3fdfd0</param>)</x-color>
    <x-color><param>#3fdfd0</param>(</x-color>define-key map <x-color><param>#fba849</param>(</x-color>kbd <x-color><param>#79a8ff</param>"C-x C-c"</x-color><x-color><param>#fba849</param>)</x-color> 'edit-server-abort<x-color><param>#3fdfd0</param>)</x-color>
    map<x-color><param>#ff62d4</param>)</x-color>
  <x-color><param>#b0d6f5</param>"Keymap for minor mode `<x-color><param>#00bcff</param>edit-server-edit-mode</x-color>'.

Redefine a few common Emacs keystrokes to functions that can
deal with the response to the edit-server request.

Any of the following keys will close the buffer and send the text
to the HTTP client: C-x #, C-c C-c.

Pressing C-x C-s will save the current state to the kill-ring.

If any of the above isused with a prefix argument, the
unmodified text is sent back instead."</x-color><x-color><param>#ffffff</param>)</x-color>

<x-color><param>#ffffff</param>(</x-color><x-color><param>#b6a0ff</param>define-minor-mode</x-color> <x-color><param>#feacd0</param>edit-server-edit-mode</x-color>
  <x-color><param>#b0d6f5</param>"Minor mode enabled on buffers opened by `<x-color><param>#00bcff</param>edit-server-accept</x-color>'.

Its sole purpose is currently to enable
`<x-color><param>#00bcff</param>edit-server-edit-mode-map</x-color>', which overrides common keystrokes to
send a response back to the client."</x-color>
  <x-color><param>#f78fe7</param>:group</x-color> 'edit-server
  <x-color><param>#f78fe7</param>:lighter</x-color> <x-color><param>#79a8ff</param>" EditSrv"</x-color>
  <x-color><param>#f78fe7</param>:init-value</x-color> nil
  <x-color><param>#f78fe7</param>:keymap</x-color> edit-server-edit-mode-map
  <x-color><param>#ff62d4</param>(</x-color><x-color><param>#b6a0ff</param>when</x-color> <x-color><param>#3fdfd0</param>(</x-color><x-color><param>#b6a0ff</param>and</x-color>
         <x-color><param>#fba849</param>(</x-color>numberp arg<x-color><param>#fba849</param>)</x-color>
         <x-color><param>#fba849</param>(</x-color>> arg 0<x-color><param>#fba849</param>)</x-color><x-color><param>#3fdfd0</param>)</x-color>
    <x-color><param>#3fdfd0</param>(</x-color>run-hooks 'edit-server-edit-mode-hook<x-color><param>#3fdfd0</param>)</x-color><x-color><param>#ff62d4</param>)</x-color><x-color><param>#ffffff</param>)</x-color>

<x-color><param>#ffffff</param>(</x-color><x-color><param>#b6a0ff</param>defun</x-color> <x-color><param>#feacd0</param>turn-on-edit-server-edit-mode-if-server</x-color> <x-color><param>#ff62d4</param>()</x-color>
  <x-color><param>#b0d6f5</param>"Turn on `<x-color><param>#00bcff</param>edit-server-edit-mode</x-color>' if in an edit-server buffer."</x-color>
  <x-color><param>#ff62d4</param>(</x-color><x-color><param>#b6a0ff</param>when</x-color> edit-server-proc
    <x-color><param>#3fdfd0</param>(</x-color>edit-server-edit-mode t<x-color><param>#3fdfd0</param>)</x-color><x-color><param>#ff62d4</param>)</x-color><x-color><param>#ffffff</param>)</x-color>

<x-color><param>#ffffff</param>(</x-color><x-color><param>#b6a0ff</param>define-globalized-minor-mode</x-color> <x-color><param>#feacd0</param>global-edit-server-edit-mode</x-color>
  edit-server-edit-mode turn-on-edit-server-edit-mode-if-server<x-color><param>#ffffff</param>)</x-color>
<x-color><param>#ffffff</param>(</x-color>global-edit-server-edit-mode t<x-color><param>#ffffff</param>)</x-color>


<x-color><param>cyan</param>;; </x-color><x-color><param>gray80</param>Edit Server socket code
</x-color>
<x-color><param>cyan</param>;; </x-color><x-color><param>gray80</param>Avoid an unnecessary prompt about active processes when exiting
</x-color><x-color><param>cyan</param>;; </x-color><x-color><param>gray80</param>emacs with no active edit-server clients
</x-color><x-color><param>cyan</param>;; </x-color><x-color><param>gray80</param>https://github.com/stsquad/emacs_chrome/issues/67
</x-color><x-color><param>cyan</param>;;</x-color><x-color><param>gray80</param>
</x-color><x-color><param>cyan</param>;; </x-color><x-color><param>gray80</param>According to http://emacswiki.org/emacs/AdviceVsHooks advice is a
</x-color><x-color><param>cyan</param>;; </x-color><x-color><param>gray80</param>sledgehammer which should be avoided if possible.  However, the
</x-color><x-color><param>cyan</param>;; </x-color><x-color><param>gray80</param>only hooks run by save-buffers-kill-emacs are defined by
</x-color><x-color><param>cyan</param>;; </x-color><x-color><param>gray80</param>kill-emacs-query-functions and run *after* the check for active
</x-color><x-color><param>cyan</param>;; </x-color><x-color><param>gray80</param>processes which results in precisely the interactive prompt we want
</x-color><x-color><param>cyan</param>;; </x-color><x-color><param>gray80</param>to avoid when edit-server has no active clients.  So it seems that
</x-color><x-color><param>cyan</param>;; </x-color><x-color><param>gray80</param>advice is the only solution until save-buffers-kill-emacs offers an
</x-color><x-color><param>cyan</param>;; </x-color><x-color><param>gray80</param>earlier hook.
</x-color><x-color><param>#ffffff</param>(</x-color><x-color><param>#b6a0ff</param>defadvice</x-color> <x-color><param>#feacd0</param>save-buffers-kill-emacs</x-color>
      <x-color><param>#ff62d4</param>(</x-color>before edit-server-stop-before-kill-emacs<x-color><param>#ff62d4</param>)</x-color>
      <x-color><param>#b0d6f5</param>"Call `<x-color><param>#00bcff</param>edit-server-stop</x-color>' if there are no active clients, to
avoid the user being prompted to kill the edit-server process."</x-color>
      <x-color><param>#ff62d4</param>(</x-color><x-color><param>#b6a0ff</param>or</x-color> edit-server-clients <x-color><param>#3fdfd0</param>(</x-color>edit-server-stop<x-color><param>#3fdfd0</param>)</x-color><x-color><param>#ff62d4</param>)</x-color><x-color><param>#ffffff</param>)</x-color>

<x-color><param>cyan</param>;;;</x-color><x-color><param>gray80</param>###<x-color><param>#dbbe5f</param>autoload</x-color>
</x-color><x-color><param>#ffffff</param>(</x-color><x-color><param>#b6a0ff</param>defun</x-color> <x-color><param>#feacd0</param>edit-server-start</x-color> <x-color><param>#ff62d4</param>(</x-color><x-color><param>#6ae4b9</param>&optional</x-color> verbose<x-color><param>#ff62d4</param>)</x-color>
  <x-color><param>#b0d6f5</param>"Start the edit server.

If argument VERBOSE is non-nil, logs all server activity to buffer
`<x-color><param>#00bcff</param>*edit-server-log*</x-color>'.  When called interactivity, a prefix argument
will cause it to be verbose."</x-color>
  <x-color><param>#ff62d4</param>(</x-color><x-color><param>#b6a0ff</param>interactive</x-color> <x-color><param>#79a8ff</param>"P"</x-color><x-color><param>#ff62d4</param>)</x-color>
  <x-color><param>#ff62d4</param>(</x-color><x-color><param>#b6a0ff</param>if</x-color> <x-color><param>#3fdfd0</param>(</x-color>process-status <x-color><param>#79a8ff</param>"edit-server"</x-color><x-color><param>#3fdfd0</param>)</x-color>
      <x-color><param>#3fdfd0</param>(</x-color>message <x-color><param>#79a8ff</param>"An edit-server process is already running"</x-color><x-color><param>#3fdfd0</param>)</x-color>
    <x-color><param>#3fdfd0</param>(</x-color><x-color><param>#b6a0ff</param>if</x-color> <x-color><param>#fba849</param>(</x-color>null <x-color><param>#9f80ff</param>(</x-color><x-color><param>#b6a0ff</param>condition-case</x-color> err
                  <x-color><param>#4fe42f</param>(</x-color><x-color><param>#b6a0ff</param>let</x-color> <x-color><param>#fe6060</param>(</x-color><x-color><param>#4fafff</param>(</x-color>proc <x-color><param>#f0dd60</param>(</x-color>make-network-process
                               <x-color><param>#f78fe7</param>:name</x-color> <x-color><param>#79a8ff</param>"edit-server"</x-color>
                               <x-color><param>#f78fe7</param>:buffer</x-color> edit-server-process-buffer-name
                               <x-color><param>#f78fe7</param>:family</x-color> 'ipv4
                               <x-color><param>#f78fe7</param>:host</x-color> <x-color><param>#ffffff</param>(</x-color><x-color><param>#b6a0ff</param>or</x-color> edit-server-host 'local<x-color><param>#ffffff</param>)</x-color>
                               <x-color><param>#f78fe7</param>:service</x-color> edit-server-port
                               <x-color><param>#f78fe7</param>:log</x-color> 'edit-server-accept
                               <x-color><param>#f78fe7</param>:server</x-color> t
                               <x-color><param>#f78fe7</param>:noquery</x-color> t<x-color><param>#f0dd60</param>)</x-color><x-color><param>#4fafff</param>)</x-color><x-color><param>#fe6060</param>)</x-color>
                    <x-color><param>#fe6060</param>(</x-color>set-process-coding-system proc 'utf-8 'utf-8<x-color><param>#fe6060</param>)</x-color>
                    proc<x-color><param>#4fe42f</param>)</x-color>
                <x-color><param>#4fe42f</param>(</x-color>file-error nil<x-color><param>#4fe42f</param>)</x-color><x-color><param>#9f80ff</param>)</x-color><x-color><param>#fba849</param>)</x-color>
        <x-color><param>#fba849</param>(</x-color>message <x-color><param>#79a8ff</param>"Failed to start an edit-server"</x-color><x-color><param>#fba849</param>)</x-color>
      <x-color><param>#fba849</param>(</x-color>ad-activate 'save-buffers-kill-emacs<x-color><param>#fba849</param>)</x-color>
      <x-color><param>#fba849</param>(</x-color><x-color><param>#b6a0ff</param>setq</x-color> edit-server-clients '<x-color><param>#9f80ff</param>()</x-color><x-color><param>#fba849</param>)</x-color>
      <x-color><param>#fba849</param>(</x-color><x-color><param>#b6a0ff</param>when</x-color> verbose <x-color><param>#9f80ff</param>(</x-color>get-buffer-create edit-server-log-buffer-name<x-color><param>#9f80ff</param>)</x-color><x-color><param>#fba849</param>)</x-color>
      <x-color><param>#fba849</param>(</x-color>edit-server-log nil <x-color><param>#79a8ff</param>"Created a new edit-server process"</x-color><x-color><param>#fba849</param>)</x-color><x-color><param>#3fdfd0</param>)</x-color><x-color><param>#ff62d4</param>)</x-color><x-color><param>#ffffff</param>)</x-color>

<x-color><param>#ffffff</param>(</x-color><x-color><param>#b6a0ff</param>defun</x-color> <x-color><param>#feacd0</param>edit-server-stop</x-color> nil
  <x-color><param>#b0d6f5</param>"Stop the edit server"</x-color>
  <x-color><param>#ff62d4</param>(</x-color><x-color><param>#b6a0ff</param>interactive</x-color><x-color><param>#ff62d4</param>)</x-color>

    <x-color><param>#3fdfd0</param>(</x-color><x-color><param>#b6a0ff</param>setq</x-color> edit-server-clients <x-color><param>#fba849</param>(</x-color>cdr edit-server-clients<x-color><param>#fba849</param>)</x-color><x-color><param>#3fdfd0</param>)</x-color><x-color><param>#ff62d4</param>)</x-color>
  <x-color><param>#ff62d4</param>(</x-color><x-color><param>#b6a0ff</param>if</x-color> <x-color><param>#3fdfd0</param>(</x-color>process-status <x-color><param>#79a8ff</param>"edit-server"</x-color><x-color><param>#3fdfd0</param>)</x-color>
      <x-color><param>#3fdfd0</param>(</x-color>delete-process <x-color><param>#79a8ff</param>"edit-server"</x-color><x-color><param>#3fdfd0</param>)</x-color>
    <x-color><param>#3fdfd0</param>(</x-color>message <x-color><param>#79a8ff</param>"No edit server running"</x-color><x-color><param>#3fdfd0</param>)</x-color><x-color><param>#ff62d4</param>)</x-color>
  <x-color><param>#ff62d4</param>(</x-color><x-color><param>#b6a0ff</param>when</x-color> <x-color><param>#3fdfd0</param>(</x-color>get-buffer edit-server-process-buffer-name<x-color><param>#3fdfd0</param>)</x-color>
    <x-color><param>#3fdfd0</param>(</x-color>kill-buffer edit-server-process-buffer-name<x-color><param>#3fdfd0</param>)</x-color><x-color><param>#ff62d4</param>)</x-color>
  <x-color><param>#ff62d4</param>(</x-color>ad-disable-advice 'save-buffers-kill-emacs
                     'before 'edit-server-stop-before-kill-emacs<x-color><param>#ff62d4</param>)</x-color>
  <x-color><param>cyan</param>;; </x-color><x-color><param>gray80</param>Disabling advice doesn't take effect until you (re-)activate
</x-color>  <x-color><param>cyan</param>;; </x-color><x-color><param>gray80</param>all advice for the function.
</x-color>  <x-color><param>#ff62d4</param>(</x-color>ad-activate 'save-buffers-kill-emacs<x-color><param>#ff62d4</param>)</x-color><x-color><param>#ffffff</param>)</x-color>

  <x-color><param>#b0d6f5</param>"Send an HTTP 200 OK response back to process PROC.
Optional second argument BODY specifies the response content:
    - If nil, the HTTP response will have null content.
    - If a string, the string is sent as response content.
    - Any other value will cause the contents of the current
      buffer to be sent.
If optional third argument progress is non-nil, then the response
will include x-file and x-open headers to allow continuation of editing."</x-color>

<x-color><param>#ffffff</param>(</x-color><x-color><param>#b6a0ff</param>defun</x-color> <x-color><param>#feacd0</param>edit-server-done</x-color> <x-color><param>#ff62d4</param>(</x-color><x-color><param>#6ae4b9</param>&optional</x-color> abort nokill<x-color><param>#ff62d4</param>)</x-color>
  <x-color><param>#b0d6f5</param>"Finish editing: send HTTP response back, close client and editing buffers.

The current contents of the buffer are sent back to the HTTP
client, unless argument ABORT is non-nil, in which case then the
original text is sent back.
If optional second argument NOKILL is non-nil, then the editing
buffer is not killed and the buffer name is passed to calling process.

When called interactively, use prefix arg to abort editing."</x-color>
  <x-color><param>#ff62d4</param>(</x-color><x-color><param>#b6a0ff</param>interactive</x-color> <x-color><param>#79a8ff</param>"P"</x-color><x-color><param>#ff62d4</param>)</x-color>
  <x-color><param>cyan</param>;; </x-color><x-color><param>gray80</param>Do nothing if the connection is closed by the browser (tab killed, etc.)

</x-color><x-color><param>cyan</param>;; </x-color><x-color><param>gray80</param>buffer back to the client which then returns new request to
</x-color><x-color><param>cyan</param>;; </x-color><x-color><param>gray80</param>continue the session). The edit-server then switches back to the
</x-color><x-color><param>cyan</param>;; </x-color><x-color><param>gray80</param>buffer referenced by the x-file header.
</x-color><x-color><param>cyan</param>;;</x-color><x-color><param>gray80</param>
</x-color>

<x-color><param>cyan</param>;;;; </x-color><x-color><param>gray80</param>Automatic fitting minor mode
</x-color><x-color><param>#ffffff</param>(</x-color><x-color><param>#b6a0ff</param>defcustom</x-color> <x-color><param>#00d3d0</param>doc-view-autofit-timer-start</x-color> 1.0

<x-color><param>#ffffff</param>(</x-color><x-color><param>#b6a0ff</param>defun</x-color> <x-color><param>#feacd0</param>doc-view-autofit-fit</x-color> <x-color><param>#ff62d4</param>()</x-color>
  <x-color><param>#b0d6f5</param>"Fits the document in the selected window's buffer
delayed with a timer, so multiple calls in succession
don't cause as much overhead."</x-color>
  <x-color><param>#ff62d4</param>(</x-color><x-color><param>#b6a0ff</param>lexical-let</x-color>
      <x-color><param>#3fdfd0</param>(</x-color><x-color><param>#fba849</param>(</x-color>window <x-color><param>#9f80ff</param>(</x-color>selected-window<x-color><param>#9f80ff</param>)</x-color><x-color><param>#fba849</param>)</x-color><x-color><param>#3fdfd0</param>)</x-color>

        <x-color><param>#fba849</param>(</x-color><x-color><param>#b6a0ff</param>setq</x-color> doc-view-autofit-timer
              <x-color><param>#9f80ff</param>(</x-color>run-with-timer
               doc-view-autofit-timer-start nil
               <x-color><param>#4fe42f</param>(</x-color><x-color><param>#b6a0ff</param>lambda</x-color> <x-color><param>#fe6060</param>()</x-color>
                 <x-color><param>#fe6060</param>(</x-color><x-color><param>#b6a0ff</param>if</x-color> <x-color><param>#4fafff</param>(</x-color>window-live-p window<x-color><param>#4fafff</param>)</x-color>
                     <x-color><param>#4fafff</param>(</x-color><x-color><param>#b6a0ff</param>save-selected-window</x-color>
                       <x-color><param>#f0dd60</param>(</x-color>select-window window<x-color><param>#f0dd60</param>)</x-color>
                       <x-color><param>#f0dd60</param>(</x-color>cancel-timer doc-view-autofit-timer<x-color><param>#f0dd60</param>)</x-color>
                       <x-color><param>#f0dd60</param>(</x-color><x-color><param>#b6a0ff</param>setq</x-color> doc-view-autofit-timer nil<x-color><param>#f0dd60</param>)</x-color>
                       <x-color><param>#f0dd60</param>(</x-color><x-color><param>#b6a0ff</param>cond</x-color>
                        <x-color><param>#ffffff</param>(</x-color><x-color><param>#ff62d4</param>(</x-color>equal 'width doc-view-autofit-type<x-color><param>#ff62d4</param>)</x-color>

                        <x-color><param>#ffffff</param>(</x-color><x-color><param>#ff62d4</param>(</x-color>equal 'height doc-view-autofit-type<x-color><param>#ff62d4</param>)</x-color>

                        <x-color><param>#ffffff</param>(</x-color><x-color><param>#ff62d4</param>(</x-color>equal 'page doc-view-autofit-type<x-color><param>#ff62d4</param>)</x-color>

      <x-color><param>#fba849</param>(</x-color>timer-inc-time doc-view-autofit-timer doc-view-autofit-timer-inc<x-color><param>#fba849</param>)</x-color><x-color><param>#3fdfd0</param>)</x-color><x-color><param>#ff62d4</param>)</x-color><x-color><param>#ffffff</param>)</x-color>

<x-color><param>#ffffff</param>(</x-color><x-color><param>#b6a0ff</param>define-minor-mode</x-color> <x-color><param>#feacd0</param>doc-view-autofit-mode</x-color>

</x-color></x-bg-color><x-color><param>#ffffff</param><x-color><param>#ffffff</param>(</x-color><x-color><param>#b6a0ff</param>defun</x-color> <x-color><param>#feacd0</param>anna-iason-time-together</x-color> <x-color><param>#ff62d4</param>()</x-color>
  <x-color><param>#b0d6f5</param>"Calculate the number of years, months, and days between the start date (Feb 16, 2017) and today's date, including both dates."</x-color>
  <x-color><param>#ff62d4</param>(</x-color><x-color><param>#b6a0ff</param>interactive</x-color><x-color><param>#ff62d4</param>)</x-color>
  <x-color><param>#ff62d4</param>(</x-color><x-color><param>#b6a0ff</param>let*</x-color> <x-color><param>#3fdfd0</param>(</x-color><x-color><param>#fba849</param>(</x-color>start-date <x-color><param>#9f80ff</param>(</x-color>encode-time 0 0 0 16 2 2017<x-color><param>#9f80ff</param>)</x-color><x-color><param>#fba849</param>)</x-color>
         <x-color><param>#fba849</param>(</x-color>end-date <x-color><param>#9f80ff</param>(</x-color>current-time<x-color><param>#9f80ff</param>)</x-color><x-color><param>#fba849</param>)</x-color>
         <x-color><param>#fba849</param>(</x-color>time-diff <x-color><param>#9f80ff</param>(</x-color>time-subtract end-date start-date<x-color><param>#9f80ff</param>)</x-color><x-color><param>#fba849</param>)</x-color>
         <x-color><param>#fba849</param>(</x-color>years <x-color><param>#9f80ff</param>(</x-color>floor <x-color><param>#4fe42f</param>(</x-color>/ <x-color><param>#fe6060</param>(</x-color>float-time time-diff<x-color><param>#fe6060</param>)</x-color> 31557600<x-color><param>#4fe42f</param>)</x-color><x-color><param>#9f80ff</param>)</x-color><x-color><param>#fba849</param>)</x-color>
         <x-color><param>#fba849</param>(</x-color>months <x-color><param>#9f80ff</param>(</x-color>floor <x-color><param>#4fe42f</param>(</x-color>/ <x-color><param>#fe6060</param>(</x-color>mod <x-color><param>#4fafff</param>(</x-color>float-time time-diff<x-color><param>#4fafff</param>)</x-color> 31557600<x-color><param>#fe6060</param>)</x-color> 2629800<x-color><param>#4fe42f</param>)</x-color><x-color><param>#9f80ff</param>)</x-color><x-color><param>#fba849</param>)</x-color>
         <x-color><param>#fba849</param>(</x-color>days <x-color><param>#9f80ff</param>(</x-color>floor <x-color><param>#4fe42f</param>(</x-color>/ <x-color><param>#fe6060</param>(</x-color>mod <x-color><param>#4fafff</param>(</x-color>float-time time-diff<x-color><param>#4fafff</param>)</x-color> 2629800<x-color><param>#fe6060</param>)</x-color> 86400<x-color><param>#4fe42f</param>)</x-color><x-color><param>#9f80ff</param>)</x-color><x-color><param>#fba849</param>)</x-color>
         <x-color><param>#fba849</param>(</x-color>total-days <x-color><param>#9f80ff</param>(</x-color>floor <x-color><param>#4fe42f</param>(</x-color>/ <x-color><param>#fe6060</param>(</x-color>float-time time-diff<x-color><param>#fe6060</param>)</x-color> 86400<x-color><param>#4fe42f</param>)</x-color><x-color><param>#9f80ff</param>)</x-color><x-color><param>#fba849</param>)</x-color><x-color><param>#3fdfd0</param>)</x-color>
    <x-color><param>#3fdfd0</param>(</x-color>message <x-color><param>#79a8ff</param>"We have been together for %d years, %d months, and %d days. (%d days in total)"</x-color> years months days total-days<x-color><param>#3fdfd0</param>)</x-color><x-color><param>#ff62d4</param>)</x-color><x-color><param>#ffffff</param>)</x-color>

</x-color></x-bg-color><x-color><param>#ffffff</param>  <x-color><param>cyan</param>;;; </x-color><x-color><param>gray80</param>Commentary:

</x-color>

<bold>* authinfo-copy-password</bold>

<x-bg-color><param>#191a1b</param><x-color><param>#a8a8a8</param>#+BEGIN_SRC emacs-lisp
</x-color></x-bg-color><x-color><param>cyan</param><x-color><param>#ffffff</param>;;; </x-color></x-color><x-color><param>#ffffff</param><x-color><param>gray80</param>authinfo.el -- hiding unnecessary passwords
</x-color><x-color><param>cyan</param>;;</x-color><x-color><param>gray80</param>
</x-color><x-color><param>cyan</param>;; </x-color><x-color><param>gray80</param>Copyright (C) 2017  Alex Schroeder <<alex@gnu.org>
</x-color><x-color><param>cyan</param>;;</x-color><x-color><param>gray80</param>
</x-color><x-color><param>cyan</param>;; </x-color><x-color><param>gray80</param>Latest version:
</x-color><x-color><param>cyan</param>;; </x-color><x-color><param>gray80</param>https://github.com/kensanata/emacs-setup/blob/master/config/authinfo-conf.el
</x-color><x-color><param>cyan</param>;;</x-color><x-color><param>gray80</param>
</x-color><x-color><param>cyan</param>;; </x-color><x-color><param>gray80</param>This program is free software: you can redistribute it and/or modify it
</x-color><x-color><param>cyan</param>;; </x-color><x-color><param>gray80</param>under the terms of the GNU General Public License as published by the Free
</x-color><x-color><param>cyan</param>;; </x-color><x-color><param>gray80</param>Software Foundation, either version 3 of the License, or (at your option)
</x-color><x-color><param>cyan</param>;; </x-color><x-color><param>gray80</param>any later version.
</x-color><x-color><param>cyan</param>;;</x-color><x-color><param>gray80</param>
</x-color><x-color><param>cyan</param>;; </x-color><x-color><param>gray80</param>This program is distributed in the hope that it will be useful, but WITHOUT
</x-color><x-color><param>cyan</param>;; </x-color><x-color><param>gray80</param>ANY WARRANTY; without even the implied warranty of MERCHANTABILITY or
</x-color><x-color><param>cyan</param>;; </x-color><x-color><param>gray80</param>FITNESS FOR A PARTICULAR PURPOSE.  See the GNU General Public License for
</x-color><x-color><param>cyan</param>;; </x-color><x-color><param>gray80</param>more details.
</x-color><x-color><param>cyan</param>;;</x-color><x-color><param>gray80</param>
</x-color><x-color><param>cyan</param>;; </x-color><x-color><param>gray80</param>You should have received a copy of the GNU General Public License along
</x-color><x-color><param>cyan</param>;; </x-color><x-color><param>gray80</param>with GNU Emacs.  If not, see <<http://www.gnu.org/licenses/>.
</x-color>
<x-color><param>cyan</param>;;; </x-color><x-color><param>gray80</param>Commentary:
</x-color><x-color><param>cyan</param>;;</x-color><x-color><param>gray80</param>
</x-color><x-color><param>cyan</param>;; </x-color><x-color><param>gray80</param>I keep some passwords in my ~/.authinfo.gpg file. When I looked for
</x-color><x-color><param>cyan</param>;; </x-color><x-color><param>gray80</param>a password, I used to open the file and search for the correct host
</x-color><x-color><param>cyan</param>;; </x-color><x-color><param>gray80</param>name (i.e. the machine name). Sometimes there were people near my
</x-color><x-color><param>cyan</param>;; </x-color><x-color><param>gray80</param>screen and I did not feel comfortable looking for passwords in such
</x-color><x-color><param>cyan</param>;; </x-color><x-color><param>gray80</param>an environment, specially if there is an entire page full of them.
</x-color><x-color><param>cyan</param>;; </x-color><x-color><param>gray80</param>So now I want to use C-c p to copy a password and have it removed
</x-color><x-color><param>cyan</param>;; </x-color><x-color><param>gray80</param>from the clipboard after a few seconds.
</x-color><x-color><param>cyan</param>;;</x-color><x-color><param>gray80</param>
</x-color><x-color><param>cyan</param>;; </x-color><x-color><param>gray80</param>Things I might want to add in the future:
</x-color><x-color><param>cyan</param>;; </x-color><x-color><param>gray80</param>- completion of hosts and usernames
</x-color>
<x-color><param>cyan</param>;;; </x-color><x-color><param>gray80</param>Code
</x-color>
<x-color><param>#ffffff</param>(</x-color><x-color><param>#b6a0ff</param>require</x-color> '<x-color><param>#00bcff</param>auth-source</x-color><x-color><param>#ffffff</param>)</x-color>
<x-color><param>#ffffff</param>(</x-color><x-color><param>#b6a0ff</param>require</x-color> '<x-color><param>#00bcff</param>cl</x-color><x-color><param>#ffffff</param>)</x-color>

<x-color><param>#ffffff</param>(</x-color>global-set-key <x-color><param>#ff62d4</param>(</x-color>kbd <x-color><param>#79a8ff</param>"C-l p"</x-color><x-color><param>#ff62d4</param>)</x-color> 'authinfo-copy-password<x-color><param>#ffffff</param>)</x-color>

<x-color><param>#ffffff</param>(</x-color><x-color><param>#b6a0ff</param>defvar</x-color> <x-color><param>#00d3d0</param>authinfo-copied-passwords</x-color> nil<x-color><param>#ffffff</param>)</x-color>

<x-color><param>#ffffff</param>(</x-color><x-color><param>#b6a0ff</param>defun</x-color> <x-color><param>#feacd0</param>authinfo-copy-password</x-color> <x-color><param>#ff62d4</param>(</x-color>host user<x-color><param>#ff62d4</param>)</x-color>
  <x-color><param>#b0d6f5</param>"Copy the password entry for a particular entry.

Remove the password from the kill ring after 10s.
The passwords to be removed are temporarily stored in
`<x-color><param>#00bcff</param>authinfo-copied-passwords</x-color>'."</x-color>
  <x-color><param>#ff62d4</param>(</x-color><x-color><param>#b6a0ff</param>interactive</x-color> <x-color><param>#3fdfd0</param>(</x-color>authinfo-prompt<x-color><param>#3fdfd0</param>)</x-color><x-color><param>#ff62d4</param>)</x-color>
  <x-color><param>#ff62d4</param>(</x-color><x-color><param>#b6a0ff</param>let</x-color> <x-color><param>#3fdfd0</param>(</x-color><x-color><param>#fba849</param>(</x-color>found <x-color><param>#9f80ff</param>(</x-color>nth 0 <x-color><param>#4fe42f</param>(</x-color>auth-source-search <x-color><param>#f78fe7</param>:host</x-color> host <x-color><param>#f78fe7</param>:user</x-color> user<x-color><param>#4fe42f</param>)</x-color><x-color><param>#9f80ff</param>)</x-color><x-color><param>#fba849</param>)</x-color><x-color><param>#3fdfd0</param>)</x-color>
    <x-color><param>#3fdfd0</param>(</x-color><x-color><param>#b6a0ff</param>when</x-color> found
      <x-color><param>#fba849</param>(</x-color><x-color><param>#b6a0ff</param>let</x-color> <x-color><param>#9f80ff</param>(</x-color><x-color><param>#4fe42f</param>(</x-color>secret <x-color><param>#fe6060</param>(</x-color>plist-get found <x-color><param>#f78fe7</param>:secret</x-color><x-color><param>#fe6060</param>)</x-color><x-color><param>#4fe42f</param>)</x-color><x-color><param>#9f80ff</param>)</x-color>

<x-bg-color><param>#101424</param><x-color><param>#aa9e9f</param>	</x-color></x-bg-color>  <x-color><param>#4fe42f</param>(</x-color><x-color><param>#b6a0ff</param>setq</x-color> secret <x-color><param>#fe6060</param>(</x-color>funcall secret<x-color><param>#fe6060</param>)</x-color><x-color><param>#4fe42f</param>)</x-color><x-color><param>#9f80ff</param>)</x-color>

<x-bg-color><param>#101424</param><x-color><param>#aa9e9f</param>	</x-color></x-bg-color><x-color><param>#9f80ff</param>(</x-color>message <x-color><param>#79a8ff</param>"Copied password for user: %s"</x-color> user<x-color><param>#9f80ff</param>)</x-color><x-color><param>#fba849</param>)</x-color><x-color><param>#3fdfd0</param>)</x-color><x-color><param>#ff62d4</param>)</x-color><x-color><param>#ffffff</param>)</x-color>

The passwords to be removed are temporarily stored in
